Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a neuroscience algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 804. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 782/5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to neuroscience structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 804/782 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on neuroscience structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 724. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 835 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the neuroscience space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 724 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 835. QED.
